# Infratex prospect research - 465 companies

Checked 27 October 2026. Scope: all rows with a published business email in the supplied workbook. This report incorporates the 250 bounded reports completed 26 October and 15 new software/AI checks. For 200 new manufacturers, the current workbook establishes category and a published business address, but no observed manual AP process; 147 official sites opened for product identity and 53 were unavailable in this pass.

All entries remain **review_needed**. A published company mailbox is not a verified named buyer address or a deliverability check. Proposed Infratex applications below are conditional hypotheses, not claims that the prospect has a problem or is using Infratex. Sources listed include workbook evidence URLs; the access line distinguishes independently opened pages from supplied-only material.

## IR27-001 - CreditLogic

**Decision:** review_needed - document workflow evidenced; current buying need and owner remain unconfirmed.  
**Campaign:** Financial software / Lending; Ireland; estimated employees 11-50.  
**Company and document evidence:** Mortgage origination extracts payslips, bank statements and valuations and generates credit packs.  
**Evidence strength:** observed; Previously checked 26 Oct 2026.  
**Likely owner and contact:** Gavin Bennett (Co-founder & CTO); target role: CTO / Head of Engineering / AI. The supplied address is info@creditlogic.com (Published company mailbox; not a named decision-maker email).  
**How Infratex could help - option 1:** Infratex could extract income, balances and valuation fields from mortgage documents into application-review records.  
**How Infratex could help - option 2:** Infratex could make mortgage evidence searchable so reviewers can trace a figure to its source.  
**Counterevidence / gaps:** CreditLogic already extracts and validates documents. Compare one unmet case rather than assume replacement demand.  
**Best qualification question:** Which borrower or financial document is hardest to process, and who owns its ingestion?  
**Sources:** https://creditlogic.com/product-mortgage-origination-software/; workbook Prospects row 6.

## IR27-014 - HAREDATA ELECTRONICS

**Decision:** review_needed - specific document intake or manual workload remains unconfirmed.  
**Campaign:** Manufacturers/distributors / Supplier invoices; United Kingdom; estimated employees 20-49.  
**Company and document evidence:** No verified supplier-invoice intake task was found in this bounded pass. Product activity alone does not establish invoice volume or manual work.  
**Evidence strength:** hypothesis_only; Previously checked 26 Oct 2026.  
**Likely owner and contact:** Named decision-maker not established (unknown); target role: Finance controller / AP manager. The supplied address is hello@haredata.co.uk (Published company mailbox; not a named decision-maker email).  
**How Infratex could help - option 1:** If supplier invoices arrive as PDFs, Infratex could extract part numbers, quantities and unit prices into an AP review table.  
**How Infratex could help - option 2:** If orders or delivery records are available, Infratex could prepare line-item comparisons and flag differences for an employee before the agreed export.  
**Counterevidence / gaps:** Confirm the finance/AP owner, invoice formats and recurring volume, current checks, and the destination system. No deployed connection is assumed.  
**Best qualification question:** Do supplier invoices or delivery notes require manual checks, at what volume, and in which system?  
**Sources:** https://www.haredataelectronics.co.uk/; https://www.europages.co.uk/HAREDATA-ELECTRONICS/00000004603019-416902001.html; workbook Prospects row 19.
